G01_STANDALONE_FIRSTFAIL |\r\n\"\"\"Drive the R15.2 queue: run every ready AGENT item, park what fails, keep going.\r\n\r\nStop policy (the only legal stops): destructive action, missing credential, mandated lane unavailable, owner-only\r\ndecision. Anything else is parked with a typed reason and the driver continues. The driver exits when no AGENT item is\r\nREADY — never because a single step failed.\r\n\"\"\"\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport json\r\nimport os\r\nimport re\r\nimport subprocess\r\nimport sys\r\nimport time\r\n\r\nR = r\"C:/Projects/Agent_Workspace/_r152_ev/_governance/round-20261022-r15_2\"\r\nQ = os.path.join(R, \"QUEUE_R15_2.json\")\r\n# A step that fails is worth exactly one automatic re-arm; beyond that the failure is a diagnosis, not a retry.\r\nATTEMPT_CAP = 3\r\nREARMABLE = (\"STEP_FAILED\",)\r\nLOG = os.path.join(R, \"receipts\", \"queue_runner.log\")\r\nWORKTREE = r\"C:/Projects/Agent_Workspace/_r152_ev\"\r\n\r\n\r\ndef load() -> dict:\r\n    return json.load(open(Q, encoding=\"utf-8\"))\r\n\r\n\r\ndef save(q: dict) -> None:\r\n    json.dump(q, open(Q, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\\n\"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\r\n    open(Q, \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\\n\").write(\"\\n\")\r\n\r\n\r\ndef log(line: str) -> None:\r\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(LOG), exist_ok=True)\r\n    with open(LOG, \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\\n\") as fh:\r\n        fh.write(line.rstrip(\"\\n\") + \"\\n\")\r\n    print(line)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef run(item: dict) -> tuple[bool, str]:\r\n    cmd = item[\"resume_cmd\"]\r\n    timeout = int(item.get(\"timeout_s\", 240))\r\n    t0 = time.time()\r\n    try:\r\n        p = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, cwd=item.get(\"cwd\") or WORKTREE, capture_output=True, text=True,\r\n                        timeout=timeout)\r\n        out, rc = (p.stdout or \"\") + (p.stderr or \"\"), p.returncode\r\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\r\n        return False, f\"TIMEOUT after {timeout}s\"\r\n    secs = round(time.time() - t0, 2)\r\n    expect = item.get(\"expect\")\r\n    ok = rc == 0 and (re.search(expect, out) is not None if expect else True)\r\n    if rc == 0 and not ok:\r\n        out = \"EXPECT_MISS (rc=0)\\n\" + out\r\n    tail = out.strip().splitlines()[-6:]\r\n    return ok, f\"rc={rc} secs={secs} expect={'hit' if ok else 'miss'}\\n    \" + \"\\n    \".join(tail)\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main() -> int:\r\n    max_steps = int(sys.argv[sys.argv.index(\"--max-steps\") + 1]) if \"--max-steps\" in sys.argv else 6\r\n    q = load()\r\n    steps = 0\r\n    log(f\"=== advance_queue start {time.strftime('%FT%T')} max_steps={max_steps} ===\")\r\n    while steps < max_steps:\r\n        # A READY-only predicate starved this queue: 3 PARTIAL and 1 DONE_RECEIPT_RED item were invisible to the\r\n        # driver forever, and a failed step became PARKED with a prose retry hint nobody read. Selection now also\r\n        # re-arms unfinished agent work, bounded by an attempt cap so a broken item cannot spin.\r\n        ready = [\r\n            i for i in q[\"items\"]\r\n            if i[\"owner_class\"] == \"AGENT\"\r\n            and i.get(\"satisfiable_by\", \"AGENT\") == \"AGENT\"\r\n            and int(i.get(\"attempts\", 0)) < ATTEMPT_CAP\r\n            and (i[\"status\"] == \"READY\"\r\n                 or (i.get(\"rearm_ok\") is True\r\n                     and i[\"status\"] in (\"PARTIAL\", \"PARKED\", \"DONE_RECEIPT_RED\")))\r\n        ]\r\n        if not ready:\r\n            log(\"no AGENT item is READY — stopping (not a failure stop)\")\r\n            break\r\n        item = ready[0]\r\n        steps += 1\r\n        item[\"status\"] = \"RUNNING\"\r\n        save(q)\r\n        log(f\"[{steps}] {item['id']} :: {item['next_action']}\")\r\n        try:\r\n            subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(R, \"tools\", \"heartbeat.py\"), item[\"id\"]],\r\n                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)\r\n        except Exception:\r\n            pass\r\n        ok, detail = run(item)\r\n        item[\"attempts\"] = int(item.get(\"attempts\", 0)) + 1\r\n        item[\"last_result\"] = detail\r\n        item[\"last_run_utc\"] = time.strftime(\"%FT%TZ\", time.gmtime())\r\n        if ok:\r\n            item[\"status\"] = \"DONE\"\r\n            log(f\"    DONE {item['id']}\")\r\n        else:\r\n            item[\"status\"] = \"PARKED\"\r\n            item[\"parked_reason\"] = \"STEP_FAILED\"\r\n            item[\"retry_hint\"] = (\"ladder: 1) narrow the command 2) raise timeout_s 3) switch arm; the item stays \"\r\n                                  \"PARKED until a human or a later pass re-arms it\")\r\n            log(f\"    PARKED {item['id']} — continuing with the next ready item\")\r\n        save(q)\r\n    q.setdefault(\"runs\", []).append({\r\n        \"at\": time.strftime(\"%FT%TZ\", time.gmtime()), \"steps\": steps,\r\n        \"done\": sum(1 for i in q[\"items\"] if i[\"status\"] == \"DONE\"),\r\n        \"parked\": sum(1 for i in q[\"items\"] if i[\"status\"] == \"PARKED\"),\r\n        \"owner\": sum(1 for i in q[\"items\"] if i[\"owner_class\"] == \"OWNER\"),\r\n        \"host\": sum(1 for i in q[\"items\"] if i[\"owner_class\"] == \"HOST\"),\r\n        \"ready\": sum(1 for i in q[\"items\"] if i[\"status\"] == \"READY\")})\r\n    save(q)\r\n    log(f\"=== advance_queue end: {json.dumps(q['runs'][-1], ensure_ascii=False)} ===\")\r\n    return 0\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    raise SystemExit(main())\r\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

A guard that can only say TERMINAL/NOT_TERMINAL forces the agent to\r\n    # choose between \"keep going forever\" and \"call an exhausted queue complete\"; both are wrong.\r\n    # The legal terminal. Without it the agent must choose between stopping illegally and claiming a completion it\r\n    # cannot have; both were measured today. Quiescence is a *fourth* claim: every obligation is either done, or\r\n    # blocked on a named principal with an issued request, and no permitted agent remedy remains.\r\n    agent_open = [i[\"id\"] for i in q[\"items\"]\r\n                  if i.get(\"satisfiable_by\", \"AGENT\") == \"AGENT\"\r\n                  and i.get(\"status\") not in (\"DONE\", \"DONE_RECEIPT_RED\")]\r\n    unmet = []\r\n    for i in q[\"items\"]:\r\n        if i.get(\"status\") == \"DONE\":\r\n            continue\r\n        if i.get(\"satisfiable_by\", \"AGENT\") == \"AGENT\":\r\n            continue\r\n        if not i.get(\"parked_reason\"):\r\n            unmet.append({\"id\": i[\"id\"], \"missing\": \"parked_reason\"})\r\n        ref = i.get(\"principal_request_ref\")\r\n        if not ref or not os.path.exists(os.path.join(R, ref)):\r\n            unmet.append({\"id\": i[\"id\"], \"missing\": \"issued principal request\"})\r\n    bypass = [i[\"id\"] for i in q[\"items\"] if i.get(\"lane_bypassed\")]\r\n    quiescent = (not agent_open) and (not unmet) and (not bypass)\r\n    claims = {\"queue_exhausted\": not blockers,\r\n              \"agent_scope_closed\": not agent_open,\r\n              \"agent_quiescent\": quiescent,\r\n              \"full_completion\": False,\r\n              \"agent_open_ids\": agent_open,\r\n              \"unmet_principal_obligations\": unmet,\r\n              \"unresolved_lane_bypass\": bypass,\r\n              \"note\": (\"queue_exhausted: no AGENT-actionable item remains; agent_scope_closed: every AGENT-satisfiable \"\r\n                       \"item is DONE; agent_quiescent: nothing is left that the agent is permitted to do, and every \"\r\n                       \"open item carries an issued request to a named principal; full_completion needs the external \"\r\n                       \"signatures, so it is false by construction\")}\r\n    if quiescent:\r\n        proof = {\"schema\": \"SBL_AGENT_QUIESCENCE_PROOF/1\", \"verdict\": \"AGENT_QUIESCENT_AWAITING_PRINCIPALS\",\r\n                 \"is_completion_claim\": False, \"claim_level\": claims,\r\n                 \"why_not_complete\": (\"agent work is exhausted; the remaining obligations need signatures the agent may \"\r\n                                      \"not produce. This is a legal stopping point, not a completion\"),\r\n                 \"round_id\": lease.get(\"round_id\", \"round-20261022-r15_2\"),\r\n                 \"observed_at_utc\": time.strftime(\"%FT%TZ\", time.gmtime())}\r\n        out = os.path.join(R, \"AGENT_QUIESCENCE_PROOF.json\")\r\n        json.dump(proof, open(out, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\\n\"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\r\n        print(json.dumps({\"verdict\": proof[\"verdict\"], \"proof\": out}, ensure_ascii=False))\r\n        return 0\r\n    if blockers:\r\n        print(json.dumps({\"verdict\": \"NOT_TERMINAL\", \"claim_level\": claims, \"blockers\": blockers},\r\n                         ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))\r\n        return 2\r\n\r\n    proof = {\"schema\": \"SBL_QUEUE_EXHAUSTION_PROOF/1\",\r\n             \"claim_level\": claims,\r\n             \"is_completion_claim\": False,\r\n             \"r15_2_complete\": False,\r\n             \"all_open_items_are_issued_requests\": True,\r\n             \"why_not_complete\": (\"queue exhaustion only means no AGENT-actionable item remains. It is not a close proof: a state name that asserts more than its predicate checks is the DELIVERY_READY defect this round already found.\")}\r\n    json.dump(proof, open(PROOF, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\\n\"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\r\n    open(PROOF, \"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\\n\").write(\"\\n\")\r\n    print(json.dumps({\"verdict\": \"QUEUE_EXHAUSTED\", \"proof\": PROOF}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))\r\n    return 0\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    raise SystemExit(main())\r\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

僅為跨域接收**參照點**，不聲稱是真正 HGK 發行 state definitions。執行確切先後、正式 enum、欄位歸屬以 Native Owner 的當期定義為準；不相容直接 `H_STAGE_MAPPING_HOLD`，不可因 SBL stage 串起就繞過 Native Gate。\r\nDOC-04#875: \r\nDOC-04#876: ## C37.6 RecoveryStateMachine：UNKNOWN_EFFECT、nonce、讀回、回滾\r\nDOC-04#877: \r\nDOC-04#878: `RUNNING → ACK_PENDING`（只送一次同 nonce）→ 若 ACK/收據與 effect 皆明→`EFFECT_OBSERVED`；超時則 `QUIESCED(no new mutation) → READBACK`，向 Owner 查 `candidate_sha+nonce+target_file/transaction id+timestamp+before/after+residue scan`。真 raw 確認 0 Effect→`KNOWN_NO_EFFECT`（要新的 Owner grant 才能再申請，**不得默默 retry**）；Raw 證明 effect 已生效→`EFFECT_OBSERVED`→Verify；無法確認→`AMBIGUOUS_HOLD`→`NATIVE_OWNER_DECISION`，不釋放重寫 lease、不重放 nonce。rollback 由唯一 Native 或合法 Local Owner 执行 `rollback_ptr` 並重新驗前狀態，沒有 rollback pointer 則 `ROLLBACK_REQUIRED` 阻擋 effect。\r\nDOC-04#879: \r\nDOC-04#880: 與 P4 `pipd-execution-contract` 精確對齊：effect intent 明確，permission token REQUIRED，at-most-once idempotency，residue scan readback，rollback pointer mandatory，retries `max=1/backoff=none` 為**最高參數上限**而非「有疑問自動嘗試一次」。未知已生效絕不能再次 Mutation。取消/Abort 依 DAG descendants 反向傳播並等待 native ack；無 ack `ABORT_PROPAGATION_INCOMPLETE`。\r\nDOC-04#881: \r\nDOC-04#882: ### C37.6.1｜QueueWorkerAPI@1／Kahn topo／取消傳播／UNKNOWN_EFFECT\r\nDOC-04#883: \r\nDOC-04#884: `TaskNode@1` = `{node_id,subject_sha,depends_on[],lane,writer_principal,write_scope[],effect_class,timeout_budget,compensation_ref,idempotency_nonce,state}`；`TaskDAG` 僅當 `len(toposort(Kahn))==len(nodes)` 且每條 `depends_on` 已存在才可接受，否則 `DAG_CYCLE` 或 `DEPENDENCY_ORPHAN`；分段 `ReadyQueue` 不會自行寫入 Repo。\r\nDOC-04#885: \r\nDOC-04#886: `enqueue(node)` 僅當 `deps=SUCCEEDED + consumerACK` 且 ScopeWriterLease 對所有 path 唯一；`claim(worker)` 回 `lease_id,expires_at,expected_preimage_sha`；`heartbeat` 只延長有效租期而不替代權限；`complete` 必交 `new_hash,raw_receipt_ref`; `fail` 只能回 `HOLD/REPAIR_REQUEST`；`cancel(parent)` 沿 DAG successors 發 `CANCEL_REQUEST`, 每子節點必回 `CANCEL_ACK`，逾 TTL 則 `CANCEL_UNCONFIRMED` + 調度停住，不准假定已撤銷先前 Effect。\r\nDOC-04#887: \r\nDOC-04#888: | Lane | 行為 | 衝突／停止判據 | 可實際核對的計算 |\r\nDOC-04#889: |---|---|---|---|\r\nDOC-04#890: | DIRECT | 一個 writer+單一 lease；順序唯一 | lease 主體 SHA 漂移→`LEASE_STALE` | enqueue A; claim A; complete A; B 因 predecessor 成功可排隊 |\r\nDOC-04#891: | KANBAN | 有界 worker 數，各有不相交 write_scope | `intersection(write_scope_A,write_scope_B)!=∅`→`JOIN_WRITE_CONFLICT` | A 寫 `src/a.py`、B 寫 `tests/b.py` 可以同時；共用 `src/a.py` 不可 |\r\nDOC-04#892: | SWARM | 非互斥讀任務可並行；Maker 和 Checker principal 固定隔離 | `maker==checker` 或 sharing secret token→`TQ_SOD` | reviewer 必有新 principal 和 PassA sealed digest |\r\nDOC-04#893: \r\nDOC-04#894: `AMBIGUOUS_HOLD` 真值：Native dispatch 超時但 Effect 結果未知，鎖原 nonce、停所有 downstream，從 HGK `operation_id` 查真 raw readback；找不到不能重送；查得已寫要核 after_sha/rollback，查得確無寫且 Owner 新 grant 方可產新 nonce。`at-most-once` 是受治理行為約束，絕非聊天重試次數。依 P4 execution-contract：`max_retries=1,backoff=none,rollback.required=true`（正式 ECP 由 PIPD/HGK 實體負責）。\r\nDOC-04#895: \r\nDOC-04#896: ## C37.7 Join 不能代替正式驗收\r\nDOC-04#897: \r\nDOC-04#898: `JoinIntegrityResult{acyclic,all_required_children_complete,same_subject,unique_writer,no_orphan,scope_ok}` 的成功只是工程完整性。`IndependentAcceptanceOracleResult` 需要 **真独立 Checker identity+execution_receipt+per-atom POS/NEG+same subject raw evidence** 才能是 PIPD／H4 資格。兩個 Worker「都說 PASS」只表示相關語言輸出，不代表獨立驗收。provider-off、TTL expired、quorum broken、writer lease mismatch、child hash 不同均取消並 read-only report。\r\nDOC-04#899: \r\nDOC-04#900: \r\nDOC-04#901: ## R05｜20 個具名既有輪子與技術槽位、補缺方法、安裝／壓製／HotSwap／退出設計\r\nDOC-04#902: \r\nDOC-04#903: **核心判準**：必須區分 `REQUIRED_CAPABILITY_SLOT`（必達能力）、`MANDATED_INTEGRATION`（必設計接線但須合法 Owner）、`DONOR_METHOD_ONLY`（使用方法不安裝）、`QUARANTINE`（可能相容但衝突待裁決）、`REJECTED`（不得選用）。外部 Repo 存在≠已安裝；License/Commit/Checksum/Host permission 缺一即 `EFFECTIVE_ENABLED=HOLD`，即使設計預設啟用。`PIPD-LS-SP` 是基座，OpenSpec/Spec Kit 等不能變成 PIPD 正式二號編譯器。\r\nDOC-04#904: \r\nDOC-04#905: ### 05.1 MethodAdmissionLedger／具名安裝優先序\r\nDOC-04#906: \r\nDOC-04#907: 1. `REQUIRED_CAPABILITY`: source intake、typed contract、scope+permit、TQAEP/evidence、Host effective-load、bounded repair，優先內建既有 PIPD/HGK/GENIEMAKER 工件；缺原生才採薄 Adapter。\r\nDOC-04#908: 2. `DESIGN_DEFAULT=ON`: `NoFitReason`，絕不可默認 `C=true`；`F` 不是「暫時還沒輪到」而是該 Mode 硬拒。\r\n863: \r\n864: ## C37.5 H0～H4 HGK Native Reader Crosswalk（不得自造 Gate 意義）\r\n865: \r\n866: | Native stage | SBL 傳入/讀取 | 正式 Owner 與請求 | Expected receipt/Stop | Effect 權限 |\r\n867: |---|---|---|---|---|\r\n868: | H0 | Source/Authority/Repo HEAD | HGK Native intake reads PIPD+RepoContext | `H0-ReceiptRef` or `SOURCE_UNBOUND` | NO by SBL |\r\n869: | H1 | scoped WorkOrder/ECP/Actor/Plan | CAPC/HGK native binder/admission | `H1-ReceiptRef` or `PERMIT_UNAVAILABLE` | Only native after grant |\r\n870: | H2 | native bounded execution/merge/transition | HGK executor and W3 owner | `H2-ReceiptRef`, readback or UNKNOWN_EFFECT | Native issuer only |\r\n871: | H3 | Raw test/evidence/rollback/claim | HGK verifier/PIPD TQAEP | `H3-ReceiptRef` or `RAW_MISSING` | Native test side effects by approved scope |\r\n872: | H4 | FrozenReadset/Checker H4 Challenge | External independent Reviewer/HGK H4 | `HGK-CHALLENGE-RECEIPT/2`, dual axes | Reviewer READ_ONLY |\r\n873: \r\n874: 此 H0～H4 僅為跨域接收**參照點**，不聲稱是真正 HGK 發行 state definitions。執行確切先後、正式 enum、欄位歸屬以 Native Owner 的當期定義為準；不相容直接 `H_STAGE_MAPPING_HOLD`，不可因 SBL stage 串起就繞過 Native Gate。\r\n875: \r\n876: ## C37.6 RecoveryStateMachine：UNKNOWN_EFFECT、nonce、讀回、回滾\r\n877: \r\n878: `RUNNING → ACK_PENDING`（只送一次同 nonce）→ 若 ACK/收據與 effect 皆明→`EFFECT_OBSERVED`；超時則 `QUIESCED(no new mutation) → READBACK`，向 Owner 查 `candidate_sha+nonce+target_file/transaction id+timestamp+before/after+residue scan`。真 raw 確認 0 Effect→`KNOWN_NO_EFFECT`（要新的 Owner grant 才能再申請，**不得默默 retry**）；Raw 證明 effect 已生效→`EFFECT_OBSERVED`→Verify；無法確認→`AMBIGUOUS_HOLD`→`NATIVE_OWNER_DECISION`，不釋放重寫 lease、不重放 nonce。rollback 由唯一 Native 或合法 Local Owner 执行 `rollback_ptr` 並重新驗前狀態，沒有 rollback pointer 則 `ROLLBACK_REQUIRED` 阻擋 effect。\r\n879: \r\n880: 與 P4 `pipd-execution-contract` 精確對齊：effect intent 明確，permission token REQUIRED，at-most-once idempotency，residue scan readback，rollback pointer mandatory，retries `max=1/backoff=none` 為**最高參數上限**而非「有疑問自動嘗試一次」。未知已生效絕不能再次 Mutation。取消/Abort 依 DAG descendants 反向傳播並等待 native ack；無 ack `ABORT_PROPAGATION_INCOMPLETE`。\r\n881: \r\n882: ### C37.6.1｜QueueWorkerAPI@1／Kahn topo／取消傳播／UNKNOWN_EFFECT\r\n883: \r\n884: `TaskNode@1` = `{node_id,subject_sha,depends_on[],lane,writer_principal,write_scope[],effect_class,timeout_budget,compensation_ref,idempotency_nonce,state}`；`TaskDAG` 僅當 `len(toposort(Kahn))==len(nodes)` 且每條 `depends_on` 已存在才可接受，否則 `DAG_CYCLE` 或 `DEPENDENCY_ORPHAN`；分段 `ReadyQueue` 不會自行寫入 Repo。\r\n885: \r\n886: `enqueue(node)` 僅當 `deps=SUCCEEDED + consumerACK` 且 ScopeWriterLease 對所有 path 唯一；`claim(worker)` 回 `lease_id,expires_at,expected_preimage_sha`；`heartbeat` 只延長有效租期而不替代權限；`complete` 必交 `new_hash,raw_receipt_ref`; `fail` 只能回 `HOLD/REPAIR_REQUEST`；`cancel(parent)` 沿 DAG successors 發 `CANCEL_REQUEST`, 每子節點必回 `CANCEL_ACK`，逾 TTL 則 `CANCEL_UNCONFIRMED` + 調度停住，不准假定已撤銷先前 Effect。\r\n887: \r\n888: | Lane | 行為 | 衝突／停止判據 | 可實際核對的計算 |\r\n889: |---|---|---|---|\r\n890: | DIRECT | 一個 writer+單一 lease；順序唯一 | lease 主體 SHA 漂移→`LEASE_STALE` | enqueue A; claim A; complete A; B 因 predecessor 成功可排隊 |\r\n891: | KANBAN | 有界 worker 數，各有不相交 write_scope | `intersection(write_scope_A,write_scope_B)!=∅`→`JOIN_WRITE_CONFLICT` | A 寫 `src/a.py`、B 寫 `tests/b.py` 可以同時；共用 `src/a.py` 不可 |\r\n892: | SWARM | 非互斥讀任務可並行；Maker 和 Checker principal 固定隔離 | `maker==checker` 或 sharing secret token→`TQ_SOD` | reviewer 必有新 principal 和 PassA sealed digest |\r\n893: \r\n894: `AMBIGUOUS_HOLD` 真值：Native dispatch 超時但 Effect 結果未知，鎖原 nonce、停所有 downstream，從 HGK `operation_id` 查真 raw readback；找不到不能重送；查得已寫要核 after_sha/rollback，查得確無寫且 Owner 新 grant 方可產新 nonce。`at-most-once` 是受治理行為約束，絕非聊天重試次數。依 P4 execution-contract：`max_retries=1,backoff=none,rollback.required=true`（正式 ECP 由 PIPD/HGK 實體負責）。\r\n895: \r\n896: ## C37.7 Join 不能代替正式驗收\r\n897: \r\n898: `JoinIntegrityResult{acyclic,all_required_children_complete,same_subject,unique_writer,no_orphan,scope_ok}` 的成功只是工程完整性。`IndependentAcceptanceOracleResult` 需要 **真独立 Checker identity+execution_receipt+per-atom POS/NEG+same subject raw evidence** 才能是 PIPD／H4 資格。兩個 Worker「都說 PASS」只表示相關語言輸出，不代表獨立驗收。provider-off、TTL expired、quorum broken、writer lease mismatch、child hash 不同均取消並 read-only report。\r\n899: \r\n900: \r\n901: ## R05｜20 個具名既有輪子與技術槽位、補缺方法、安裝／壓製／HotSwap／退出設計\r\n902: \r\n903: **核心判準**：必須區分 `REQUIRED_CAPABILITY_SLOT`（必達能力）、`MANDATED_INTEGRATION`（必設計接線但須合法 Owner）、`DONOR_METHOD_ONLY`（使用方法不安裝）、`QUARANTINE`（可能相容但衝突待裁決）、`REJECTED`（不得選用）。外部 Repo 存在≠已安裝；License/Commit/Checksum/Host permission 缺一即 `EFFECTIVE_ENABLED=HOLD`，即使設計預設啟用。`PIPD-LS-SP` 是基座，OpenSpec/Spec Kit 等不能變成 PIPD 正式二號編譯器。\r\n904: \r\n905: ### 05.1 MethodAdmissionLedger／具名安裝優先序\r\n906: \r\n907: 1. `REQUIRED_CAPABILITY`: source intake、typed contract、scope+permit、TQAEP/evidence、Host effective-load、bounded repair，優先內建既有 PIPD/HGK/GENIEMAKER 工件；缺原生才採薄 Adapter。\r\n908: 2. `DESIGN_DEFAULT=ON`: PIPD P4 eight skills 與本地已提供 CAPC/challenge-review/GENIEMAKER **作設計能力**；不推導 Runner Enabled。Spec Kit `v1.0.13` 和 Superpowers `v6.4.2` 是 **研究可驗 pinned version candidate**，不是裝置觀測實值。Matt plugin manifest 為 `v1.3.1`（source observation）但實體 import SHA/License 要重新驗。\r\n909: 3. `OPEN_SPEC`、`GSD`、`BMAD`、`gstack` 的用法按本檔下列 Ledger/Owner review，不自動 install，尤其 gstack 上游出現 QUARANTINE；保留角色工作流為方法論 donor。\r\nSBL_PIPD-LS-PKG_DOC-01.md#873: | U14.2 | U14 | OPS ECP | TPM 具名對應 OPS ECP、DEV ECP 分別 | SBL_PIPD-LS-PKG_全DOC藍圖(8).md:L3715@8e8327dbc53be8a1722d1cd5cdafd240cd9bb358218efaae13689827b80986cb | `JoinIntegrityResult{acyclic,all_required_children_complete,same_subject,unique_writer,no_orphan,scope_ok}` 的成功只是工程完整性。`IndependentAcceptanceOracleResult` 需要 **真独立 Checker identity+execution_receipt+per-atom POS/NEG+same subject raw evidence** 才能是 PIPD／H4 資格。兩個 Worker「都說 PASS」只表示相關語言輸出，不代表獨立驗收。provider-off、TTL expired、quorum broken、writer lease mismatch、child hash 不同均取消並 read-only report。\r\n899: \r\n900: \r\n901: ## R05｜20 個具名既有輪子與技術槽位、補缺方法、安裝／壓製／HotSwap／退出設計\r\n902: \r\n903: **核心判準**：必須區分 `REQUIRED_CAPABILITY_SLOT`（必達能力）、`MANDATED_INTEGRATION`（必設計接線但須合法 Owner）、`DONOR_METHOD_ONLY`（使用方法不安裝）、`QUARANTINE`（可能相容但衝突待裁決）、`REJECTED`（不得選用）。外部 Repo 存在≠已安裝；License/Commit/Checksum/Host permission 缺一即 `EFFECTIVE_ENABLED=HOLD`，即使設計預設啟用。`PIPD-LS-SP` 是基座，OpenSpec/Spec Kit 等不能變成 PIPD 正式二號編譯器。\r\n904: \r\n905: ### 05.1 MethodAdmissionLedger／具名安裝優先序\r\n906: \r\n907: 1. `REQUIRED_CAPABILITY`: source intake、typed contract、scope+permit、TQAEP/evidence、Host effective-load、bounded repair，優先內建既有 PIPD/HGK/GENIEMAKER 工件；缺原生才採薄 Adapter。\r\n908: 2. `DESIGN_DEFAULT=ON`: PIPD P4 eight skills 與本地已提供 CAPC/challenge-review/GENIEMAKER **作設計能力**；不推導 Runner Enabled。Spec Kit `v1.0.13` 和 Superpowers `v6.4.2` 是 **研究可驗 pinned version candidate**，不是裝置觀測實值。Matt plugin manifest 為 `v1.3.1`（source observation）但實體 import SHA/License 要重新驗。\r\n909: 3. `OPEN_SPEC`、`GSD`、`BMAD`、`gstack` 的用法按本檔下列 Ledger/Owner review，不自動 install，尤其 gstack 上游出現 QUARANTINE；保留角色工作流為方法論 donor。\r\n藍圖資料庫\\SBL｜三方協作_升級藍圖 lane:\r\n            continue\r\n        a = (d.get(\"attempts\") or [{}])[0]\r\n        out.setdefault(lane, []).append({\"file\": os.path.basename(f), \"ok\": bool(d.get(\"ok\")),\r\n                                         \"nonce\": bool(a.get(\"nonce_present\")),\r\n                                         \"model\": d.get(\"used_transport_model\"),\r\n                                         \"secs\": a.get(\"seconds\")})\r\n    return out\r\n\r\n\r\ndef check(role: str) -> int:\r\n    m = json.load(open(MANDATE, encoding=\"utf-8\"))\r\n    spec = m[\"roles\"].get(role)\r\n    if not
